---
title: Icons
sidebar_label: Icons
---

# Icons

Every component property that draws a glyph — `icon`, `iconOn`, `iconOff` and
`trailingIcon` — takes a [Material Symbols](https://fonts.google.com/icons)
ligature name. The value is a bare `.goi` identifier: `icon: add`, not a path
and not a codepoint.

The names below are read from the Material Symbols font that ships inside goi, so
this list and what the renderer can draw cannot drift. A name that is not here
draws nothing. The language server completes these names in an icon property.

```goi
Button { label: "Save" icon: check }
Switch { label: "Dark mode" iconOn: dark_mode iconOff: light_mode }
ListItem { label: "Inbox" icon: inbox trailingIcon: chevron_right }
```

**Tip:** the page is long — use your browser's find (Ctrl+F / Cmd+F) to jump
straight to a name.
